headers at H-L\nDATA_HEADER_ROW=4\nDATA_FIRST_DATA_ROW=21\nDATA_LAST_DATA_ROW=38\nSERIES_CODE_COL=4  # D\nFIRST_YEAR_COL=8   # H\nLAST_YEAR_COL=12   # L\n\n# Validate expected year headers # 35->26\n    for c in range(FIRST_YEAR_COL, LAST_YEAR_COL+1):\n        cell=ws_task.cell(r,c)\n        if cell.value not in (None, ''):\n            raise SystemExit(f\"Refusing to overwrite Task!{get_column_letter(c)}{r} which already has {cell.value!r}\")\n        col_letter=get_column_letter(c)\n        formula=f\"=({col_letter}{preserved_row}-{col_letter}{consumed_row})/{col_letter}{cap_row}*100\"\n        cell.value=formula\n\n# ---- Step 4: Summary statistics ----\n# Rows 42-47 correspond to 'PERCENTILE.INC',\n}\nfor c in range(FIRST_YEAR_COL, LAST_YEAR_COL+1):\n    col_letter=get_column_letter(c)\n    rng=f\"{col_letter}35:{col_letter}40\"\n    for r in range(42,48):\n        cell=ws_task.cell(r,c)\n        if cell.value not in (None, ''):\n            raise SystemExit(f\"Refusing to overwrite Task!{col_letter}{r} which already has {cell.value!r}\")\n        fn=stat_row_to_formula[r]\n        if r==46:\n            cell.value=f\"={fn}({rng},0.25)\"\n        elif r==47:\n            cell.value=f\"={fn}({rng},0.75)\"\n        else:\n            cell.value=f\"={fn}({rng})\"\n\n# ---- Step 5: Weighted mean ----\nfor c in range(FIRST_YEAR_COL, LAST_YEAR_COL+1):\n    col_letter=get_column_letter(c)\n    cell=ws_task.cell(50,c)\n    if cell.value weights_rng=f\"{col_letter}26:{col_letter}31\"\n    cell.value=f\"=SUMPRODUCT({values_rng},{weights_rng})/SUM({weights_rng})\"\n\nwb.save(out_path)\nprint('Saved:', out_path)\nPY","description":"Write
